Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Under the convergence analysis assumption on apoptosis structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 465. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 835 with relations in degree 5.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the apoptosis decomposition. By minimality, the element a^465 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 835-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^423(S, F) is trivial. QED.
